# `pcloud-fs`

**Maturity:** Evolving product surface

**Version:** `0.1.0`

**Directory:** `crates/pcloud-fs`

**Manifest:** [`crates/pcloud-fs/Cargo.toml`](https://github.com/ezechiel203/pcloud-rs/blob/main/crates/pcloud-fs/Cargo.toml)

Cross-platform mounted-drive integration, staging, and crash-safe writeback for pcloud-rs.

## Feature-family profile

**Why it exists.** Translate remote-drive semantics into native filesystem behavior while surviving caching, partial writes, crashes, and platform ABI differences.

**What it is good for.** Linux/BSD FUSE, macOS fuse-t/macFUSE, Windows WinFSP, read caching, staged writeback, journals, orphan cleanup, and filesystem watching.

**Why it is good at that job.** A portable adapter core plus narrow native shims, stable inode mapping, bounded caches, and write-ahead staging protect correctness across kernels.
